1. What is the main idea of the passage?

  • A) Cisplatin is an ineffective drug against triple-negative breast cancer.
  • B) CSC-derived exosomal miRNAs may actively contribute to the spread of chemoresistance in breast tumors.
  • C) Exosomes are larger than most extracellular vesicles.
  • D) All breast cancer cells respond identically to cisplatin.
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: ایده اصلی در جمله‌های پایانی آمده: «exosomal miRNAs ... not merely as passive biomarkers but as functional players» — گزینه ۱ با متن تناقض دارد (اثربخشی cisplatin رد نشده)، گزینه ۳ جزئیات اشتباه و فرعی است، گزینه ۴ با «cell-type-specific manner» تناقض دارد.
استراتژی: ایده اصلی معمولاً در جمله concluding آخرین پاراگراف یا جمله اول پاراگراف اول است. گزینه‌های جزئی (مثل عدد و اندازه) هرگز ایده اصلی نیستند.

2. According to the passage, what is the approximate size range of exosomes?

  • A) 18–24 nanometers
  • B) 30–150 nanometers
  • C) 150–300 nanometers
  • D) 30–150 micrometers
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله دوم پاراگراف دوم: «ranging from 30 to 150 nanometers in diameter». گزینه ۱ عدد مربوط به اندازه miRNA (نوکلئوتید) است — تله رایج جابه‌جایی اعداد.
استراتژی: سوالات عددی: عدد را با واحدش دقیق چک کنید؛ طراحان معمولاً اعداد دو مفهوم مختلف را جابه‌جا می‌کنند.

3. The word "cargo" in paragraph 2 is closest in meaning to:

  • A) waste products
  • B) protective coating
  • C) transported content
  • D) membrane receptor
پاسخ: گزینه C
توضیح: بعد از cargo یک dash توضیحی آمده: «—including microRNAs...— between cells» یعنی محتوایی که بین سلول‌ها حمل می‌شود. واژه‌شناسی از بافت (context clues).
استراتژی: هرگاه بعد از واژه علامت — یا such as/including آمد، خود متن دارد واژه را تعریف می‌کند.

4. It can be inferred from the passage that:

  • A) parental and CSC-like cells package identical miRNAs into exosomes
  • B) intercellular communication may play a role in treatment failure
  • C) miRNAs regulate gene expression at the transcriptional level
  • D) exosomal miRNAs have no clinical potential
پاسخ: گزینه B

استراتژی: در سوالات inference، گزینه‌ای که «مستقیماً با یک جمله متن تناقض دارد» فوراً حذف می‌شود.

5. Which of the following is NOT stated in the passage?

  • A) CSCs are capable of self-renewal.
  • B) Cisplatin changed the exosomal miRNA profile in a cell-type-specific manner.

استراتژی: در سوالات NOT stated، گزینه‌ای با ادعای قوی‌تر از متن (تأیید شده، درمان قطعی، اثبات کامل) معمولاً جواب است.

The ceRNA Hypothesis: lncRNA–miRNA–mRNA Networks in Breast Cancer

بر اساس مقاله: Datasets exploring putative lncRNA-miRNA-mRNA axes in breast cancer cell lines (Data in Brief, 2021)

For decades, molecular biology focused on protein-coding genes, which constitute less than two percent of the human genome. The advent of high-throughput transcriptomics, however, revealed that the majority of the genome is transcribed into non-coding RNAs. Among these, long non-coding RNAs (lncRNAs), defined as transcripts longer than 200 nucleotides lacking protein-coding potential, have attracted considerable attention in cancer research. A influential model proposed to explain their function is the competing endogenous RNA (ceRNA) hypothesis. According to this framework, lncRNAs possess miRNA response elements (MREs)—short sequences complementary to microRNAs—and can therefore act as molecular sponges, sequestering miRNAs away from their natural mRNA targets. When an lncRNA absorbs a miRNA that would ordinarily suppress an oncogenic mRNA, the expression of that mRNA is effectively de-repressed. In breast cancer cell lines, computational analyses have mapped thousands of putative lncRNA–miRNA–mRNA axes, many of which converge on key pathways such as PI3K-AKT signaling, epithelial–mesenchymal transition (EMT), and apoptosis. Experimental validation, however, lags far behind bioinformatic prediction, and researchers caution that sponge effects require specific abundance ratios that may exist only in certain cellular contexts. Nevertheless, the ceRNA model provides a unifying language for describing cross-talk among transcript classes and continues to guide the discovery of diagnostic biomarkers and therapeutic candidates.
واژگان کلیدی:
high-throughputتوان‌بالا؛ فناوری‌های پردازش موازی انبوه نمونه‌ها
lncRNARNA بلند غیرکدکننده؛ رونوشت >۲۰۰ نوکلئوتید بدون ساخت پروتئین
ceRNA (competing endogenous RNA)RNA درون‌زاد رقیب؛ مدل اسفنجی رقابت بر سر miRNA
molecular spongeاسفنج مولکولی؛ مولکولی که miRNA را به خود می‌گیرد
sequesterجداسازی/به‌دام‌انداختن؛ جذب و نگه‌داشتن miRNA
de-repressedآزاد از سرکوب؛ بیان مجدد پس از رفع مهار
EMTگذار اپیتلیال-مزانشیمی؛ گام کلیدی تهاجم و متاستاز
putativeاحتمالی/فرضی؛ پیشنهادشده اما تأییدنشده

1. The primary purpose of the passage is to:

  • A) prove that lncRNAs are translated into proteins
  • B) introduce the ceRNA hypothesis and describe its relevance and limitations in breast cancer research
  • C) criticize high-throughput methods as unreliable
  • D) list all miRNAs expressed in breast cancer
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: ساختار متن: معرفی lncRNA → توضیح مدل ceRNA → داده‌های سرطان پستان → محدودیت‌ها → جمع‌بندی. هدفِ «معرفی + نقش + محدودیت» در گزینه ۲ بازتاب کامل دارد.
استراتژی: سوال purpose/ساختار: پاسخ باید «کل» متن را پوشش دهد؛ گزینه‌هایی که فقط یک پاراگراف را می‌پوشانند یا با متن تناقض دارند حذف می‌شوند.

2. According to the ceRNA hypothesis, an oncogenic mRNA is de-repressed when:

  • A) the miRNA that targets it is sequestered by a competing lncRNA
  • B) the lncRNA is degraded by the miRNA
  • C) the mRNA acquires new miRNA response elements
  • D) the PI3K-AKT pathway is inhibited
پاسخ: گزینه A
توضیح: جمله کلیدی: «lncRNAs ... act as molecular sponges, sequestering miRNAs away from their natural mRNA targets» → miRNA که سرکوب‌کننده mRNA انکوژنیک بود، توسط lncRNA به دام می‌افتد و mRNA آزاد می‌شود.
استراتژی: سوالات مکانیسم/علت-مععلول: زنجیره رویداد را با فلش در حاشیه بنویسید: lncRNA ← miRNA ← mRNA آزاد.

3. The phrase "lags far behind" in the last paragraph suggests that:

  • A) bioinformatic predictions are rarely published
  • B) experimental confirmation is much less advanced than computational prediction
  • C) researchers refuse to validate the hypothesis
  • D) breast cancer research is underfunded
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: lag behind = عقب‌ماندن؛ بین prediction (انبوه) و validation (کم) شکاف وجود دارد. گزینه ۳ و ۴ اطلاعات خارج از متن‌اند.
استراتژی: عبارات مقایسه‌ای (lag behind, far behind) معمولاً مقایسه دو چیز در متن را می‌سنجند — دو طرف مقایسه را پیدا کنید.

4. The author mentions the requirement of "specific abundance ratios" in order to:

  • A) strengthen the claim that all ceRNA interactions are universal
  • B) qualify the ceRNA hypothesis by noting context-dependent limitations
  • C) introduce a new drug target
  • D) dismiss the ceRNA hypothesis entirely

استراتژی: در سوالات explicitly named/stated، فقط به متن رجوع کنید؛ دانش قبلی شما درباره مسیرهای دیگر منشأ خطاست.

Functional Analysis of miRNA Data: Bioinformatic Tools

بر اساس مقاله: Computational Methods and Software Tools for Functional Analysis of miRNA Data (Biomolecules, 2020)

Because a single microRNA can regulate hundreds of target genes, and a single gene may be regulated by dozens of microRNAs, interpreting miRNA expression data poses a formidable analytical challenge. MicroRNAs rarely act in isolation; instead, they operate within coordinated regulatory networks. To extract biological meaning from lists of differentially expressed miRNAs, researchers rely heavily on enrichment analysis. This family of methods asks whether genes associated with a particular biological term—such as a pathway, molecular function, or disease—are over-represented among the targets of the observed miRNAs compared with what would be expected by chance. The statistical foundation of most enrichment tools is the hypergeometric distribution, often coupled with correction for multiple hypothesis testing, such as the Benjamini–Hochberg procedure, which controls the false discovery rate. Despite their utility, target-prediction algorithms suffer from high false-positive rates, since miRNA targeting depends not only on seed complementarity but also on target-site accessibility, RNA-binding proteins, and cellular context. Consequently, integrated platforms now combine several prediction databases, incorporate experimentally validated interactions from resources such as TarBase, and allow users to filter results by expression consistency. Reviewers of these tools emphasize that the choice of background gene set—the universe of genes against which enrichment is computed—profoundly influences the outcome, and that an inappropriate background can generate entirely spurious conclusions. Careful experimental design and domain knowledge therefore remain indispensable even in the era of one-click bioinformatics.
واژگان کلیدی:
over-representedبیش‌نمایی‌شده؛ فراوان‌تر از انتظار تصادفی
enrichment analysisآنالیز غنای ژنی؛ آزمون فراوانی غیرتصادفی مقوله‌های بیولوژیک
hypergeometric distributionتوزیع فوق‌هندسی؛ مبنای آماری آزمون غنا
false discovery rate (FDR)نرخ کشف کاذب؛ کنترل خطای آزمون‌های متعدد
seed complementarityمکمل‌بودن ناحیه seed میکروRNA با هدف
target-site accessibilityدسترسی‌پذیری جایگاه هدف روی mRNA
background gene setمجموعه ژن‌های زمینه برای مقایسه در آزمون غنا
spuriousکاذب/بی‌اعتبار؛ ظاهراً معتبر اما غلط

1. Why is interpreting miRNA data described as a "formidable challenge"?

  • A) because miRNAs act completely independently of each other
  • B) because of the many-to-many relationship between miRNAs and their target genes
  • C) because miRNAs are too long to sequence
  • D) because expression data cannot be measured
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله اول: یک miRNA صدها هدف و یک ژن ده‌ها miRNA دارد → رابطه many-to-many. گزینه ۱ دقیقاً برعکس متن (in isolation را متن رد می‌کند) است.
استراتژی: صفت‌های قوی مثل formidable همیشه با دلیل در جمله بعدی همراه‌اند — دلیل را پیدا کنید.

  • B) controlling the false discovery rate
  • C) validating target sites experimentally
  • D) normalizing expression values

توضیح: «...correction for multiple hypothesis testing, such as the Benjamini–Hochberg procedure, which controls the false discovery rate» — همان جمله تعریف را می‌دهد.
استراتژی: اسامی خاص (روش/دانشمند) معمولاً در سوال با کارکردشان پرسیده می‌شوند؛ جمله حاوی نام را کامل بخوانید.

3. According to the passage, target prediction has high false-positive rates because:

  • A) seed sequences are always incorrect

  • C) hypergeometric tests are unavailable

استراتژی: عبارت not only...but also ساختار «دلیل چندبخشی» می‌سازد؛ گزینه درست همه اجزا را دارد، گزینه انحرافی فقط یک جزء مطلق‌سازی‌شده.

4. The author's attitude toward "one-click bioinformatics" can best be described as:

  • A) enthusiastically supportive
  • B) cautiously skeptical
  • C) openly hostile
  • D) completely indifferent

5. All of the following are stated about the background gene set EXCEPT:

  • A) it influences enrichment results profoundly
  • B) an inappropriate background can lead to spurious conclusions
  • C) it should always include every gene in the genome
  • D) it is the universe of genes used for comparison
پاسخ: گزینه C
توضیح: متن تعریف و اهمیت پس‌زمینه را گفته اما هرگز گفته «باید همه ژنوم باشد» — این ادعا اضافه‌شده است (EXCEPT).
استراتژی: در سوالات EXCEPT، سه گزینه paraphrase مستقیم متن‌اند؛ گزینه دارای قید مطلق (always/every) معمولاً جواب است.

Early Detection of Breast Cancer: Principles of Screening

بر اساس کتاب: Early Diagnosis and Treatment of Cancer: Breast Cancer (Saunders/Elsevier)

The rationale for early detection of breast cancer rests on a simple observation: tumors confined to the breast are associated with substantially better survival than tumors that have already spread to regional lymph nodes or distant organs. Screening aims to detect a disease in its preclinical phase—that is, before it produces symptoms—through the systematic application of a test to an apparently healthy population. For a screening program to be justified, several criteria should be satisfied: the disease must be an important health problem with a recognizable early stage; early treatment must offer a benefit over late treatment; and the test must be acceptable, safe, affordable, and sufficiently accurate. Mammography, the reference screening modality, reduces breast cancer mortality in women invited to screening by roughly 20 percent, although debate persists regarding overdiagnosis—the detection of indolent tumors that would never have become clinically apparent. Supplemental screening with magnetic resonance imaging is recommended for women at high genetic risk, such as BRCA mutation carriers, whose dense breast tissue and earlier disease onset reduce mammographic sensitivity. Clinical breast examination and breast self-examination, once promoted widely, now play a limited role because randomized trials failed to demonstrate a mortality reduction. Risk-stratified screening, in which the intensity and modality of surveillance are tailored to individual risk profiles—incorporating polygenic scores, mammographic density, and family history—represents the emerging paradigm, shifting the field from a one-size-fits-all approach toward personalized detection strategies.
واژگان کلیدی:
preclinical phaseفاز پیش‌بالینی؛ مرحله قبل از بروز علائم
prevalence vs. incidenceشیوع vs. بروز (تعداد کل موارد در مقابل موارد جدید)
overdiagnosisتشخیص بیش‌ازحد؛ کشف تومورهای کم‌خطر که هرگز علامت‌دار نمی‌شدند
indolentکم‌خطر/آهسته؛ تومور با رشد کند

mortality reductionکاهش مرگ‌ومیر؛ معیار اصلی موفقیت غربالگری
risk-stratifiedطبقه‌بندی‌شده بر اساس ریسک
polygenic scoreامتیاز پلی‌ژنیک؛ مجموع وزن‌دار واریانت‌های ریسک

1. According to the passage, the fundamental justification for early detection is that:

  • A) all tumors eventually metastasize
  • B) localized tumors have considerably better outcomes than disseminated ones
  • C) screening eliminates the need for treatment
  • D) mammography has no limitations
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله اول: «tumors confined to the breast are associated with substantially better survival...» — گزینه‌های مطلق‌سازی‌شده (all/eliminates/no) با متن نمی‌خوانند.
استراتژی: گزینه‌های دارای all/never/no/eliminate تقریباً همیشه غلط‌اند مگر متن صریحاً بگوید.

2. The word "indolent" is closest in meaning to:

  • A) aggressive
  • B) slow-growing
  • C) invisible on imaging
  • D) hormone-sensitive
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: تعریف داخل همان جمله آمده: «tumors that would never have become clinically apparent» → رشد بسیار کند. واژگان overdiagnosis همیشه با indolent همراه است.
استراتژی: indolent در متون انکولوژی = کم‌خطر و آهسته؛ این واژه در آزمون‌های قبلی بارها آمده است — حفظ کنید.

3. Why is supplemental MRI recommended for BRCA mutation carriers?

  • A) because MRI is cheaper than mammography
  • B) because dense tissue and earlier onset reduce the sensitivity of mammography in these women
  • C) because BRCA tumors do not appear on mammography at all
  • D) because MRI replaces genetic testing
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «whose dense breast tissue and earlier disease onset reduce mammographic sensitivity». گزینه ۳ مطلق‌سازی غلط (کاهش حساسیت ≠ دیده‌نشدن مطلق).
استراتژی: پاساژهای غربالگری الگوی ثابت دارند: «در گروه پرخطر، تست مکمل به‌دلیل محدودیت تست اصلی» — این الگو را به خاطر بسپارید.

4. It can be inferred that breast self-examination lost prominence because:

  • A) it is too expensive for public programs
  • B) randomized trials did not show reduced mortality
  • C) women refused to perform it
  • D) imaging replaced all physical examinations
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «randomized trials failed to demonstrate a mortality reduction» — عین متن. معیار طلایی ارزش‌گذاری هر آزمون غربالگری = کاهش مرگ‌ومیر در کارآزمایی.
استراتژی: در متون غربالگری، جمله «کارآزمایی تصادفی نشان داد/نشان نداد» همیشه سوژه سوال است.

5. The emerging paradigm described in the passage is:

  • A) uniform screening for all women at the same age
  • B) abandoning mammography entirely
  • C) tailoring screening intensity and modality to individual risk
  • D) screening only symptomatic women
پاسخ: گزینه C
توضیح: پاراگراف پایانی: «Risk-stratified screening ... tailored to individual risk profiles» — گزینه ۱ برعکسِ one-size-fits-all است که متن آن را کنار می‌گذارد.
استراتژی: پاساژهای علمی جدید اغلب در جمله آخر «پارادایم نوظهور» را معرفی می‌کنند؛ اگر سوال آخر ریدینگ درباره آینده/روند باشد، جمله پایانی را ببینید.

زبان تخصصی — بخش دوم: آناتومی پستان، اپیدمیولوژی و درمان‌های نوین

The Normal Breast and Benign Breast Disease

بر اساس فصل: The Normal Breast and Benign Diseases of the Breast

The functional unit of the female breast is the terminal duct lobular unit (TDLU), which consists of the terminal duct and its associated alveoli. The entire ductal system is lined by two cell layers: an inner layer of epithelial cells responsible for milk secretion and an outer layer of myoepithelial cells whose contractile properties propel secretions toward the nipple. This dual layering has diagnostic significance, because invasive carcinomas are characterized by the loss of myoepithelial cells, whereas benign in situ proliferations retain them. Most benign breast conditions reflect exaggerated responses of the TDLU to hormonal stimulation. Fibroadenomas, the most common benign neoplasms in young women, are composed of both stromal and epithelial components and typically present as mobile, well-circumscribed masses. Sclerosing adenosis, a proliferation of distorted acini within fibrotic stroma, may mimic carcinoma both clinically and radiologically, frequently containing microcalcifications that warrant biopsy. Fat necrosis, often following trauma or surgery, produces a firm irregular mass that can closely resemble malignancy. Similarly, chronic mastitis and inflammatory carcinoma may be indistinguishable on clinical grounds alone, underscoring the essential role of biopsy in the evaluation of any dominant breast mass. Ectopic breast tissue, most commonly in the axilla, may enlarge during pregnancy and raise unfounded concern for metastasis.
واژگان کلیدی:
terminal duct lobular unit (TDLU)واحد ترمینال دکتال-لوبولار؛ واحد عملکردی پستان
myoepithelial cellsسلول‌های میواپیتلیال؛ لایه بیرونی با خاصیت انقباضی
in situدرجا؛ محدود به محل خود بدون تهاجم

microcalcificationsمیکروکلسیفیکاسیون‌ها؛ ریز رسوبات کلسیم در ماموگرافی

1. Why is the two-cell-layer structure of the ductal system diagnostically important?

  • A) because myoepithelial cells secrete milk
  • B) because loss of myoepithelial cells marks invasive carcinoma while benign in situ lesions retain them
  • C) because epithelial cells are absent in benign tumors
  • D) because the layers determine the side of the tumor
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله سوم صریحاً: «invasive carcinomas are characterized by the loss of myoepithelial cells, whereas benign in situ proliferations retain them» — نکته هیستوپاتولوژیک کلیدی.
استراتژی: عبارت diagnostic significance در متن علامت است که جمله بعد، سوژه سوال است.

2. Which benign lesion most likely requires biopsy because of its radiologic appearance?

  • A) fibroadenoma
  • B) sclerosing adenosis
  • C) ectopic breast tissue
  • D) normal TDLU
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «Sclerosing adenosis ... may mimic carcinoma both clinically and radiologically, frequently containing microcalcifications that warrant biopsy»؛ mimic carcinoma = شبیه بدخیمی → نیاز به بیوپسی.
استراتژی: دو واژه mimic و warrant را بشناسید: mimic = شبیه‌سازی بدخیمی؛ warrant = ایجاب کردن/موجب شدن (اقدام).

3. The word "propel" in paragraph 1 is closest in meaning to:

  • A) inhibit
  • B) push forward
  • C) digest
  • D) transform
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: سلول میواپیتلیال با انقباض ترشحات را به سمت نوک پستان «به جلو می‌راند». ریشه فیزیکی propel = پیش‌رانندگی.
استراتژی: همیشه وظیفه بیولوژیک ساختار را در توضیح واژه لحاظ کنید: myo- (عضله) + propel (راندن).

4. It can be inferred that a firm irregular breast mass:

  • A) is always malignant
  • B) may result from fat necrosis and still require biopsy for clarification
  • C) never warrants imaging
  • D) is exclusive to inflammatory carcinoma
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «Fat necrosis ... produces a firm irregular mass that can closely resemble malignancy» + «underscoring the essential role of biopsy» → استنباط: بیوپسی لازم است حتی اگر علت خوش‌خیم محتمل باشد. گزینه always مطلق و غلط.
استراتژی: استنباط معکوس: هرچه «همیشه/هرگز» دارد غلط؛ هرچه با منطق احتیاط بالینی (بیوپسی/پیگیری) سازگار است درست.

5. Enlarging axillary breast tissue during pregnancy:

  • A) indicates metastasis and requires mastectomy
  • B) is a typical behavior of ectopic breast tissue and usually benign
  • C) results from chronic mastitis
  • D) represents a fibroadenoma
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله آخر: «Ectopic breast tissue, most commonly in the axilla, may enlarge during pregnancy and raise unfounded concern for metastasis» — unfounded = بی‌پایه؛ یعنی نگرانی بی‌مورد است.
استراتژی: واژه unfounded/baseless در متن یعنی متن خودش نگرانی را رد می‌کند — گزینه‌های تشدیدکننده نگرانی غلط‌اند.

Cancer Epidemiology in Iran: A National Profile

بر اساس: GLOBOCAN Fact Sheet — Iran (Islamic Republic of), 2022

According to the most recent global estimates, approximately 137,000 new cancer cases and 87,000 cancer deaths occur annually in Iran. Cancer of the stomach ranks first in men, followed by prostate and lung cancer, whereas breast cancer is by far the most common malignancy in women, followed by stomach and colorectal cancers. The age-standardized incidence rate in men (about 168 per 100,000) exceeds that in women (about 129 per 100,000), and the cumulative risk of developing cancer before age 75 approaches 18 percent in men. Several features distinguish the Iranian cancer profile from Western patterns. First, the relatively young age structure of the population means that a substantial proportion of patients are diagnosed during their productive years, amplifying the societal burden of disease. Second, infection-related malignancies such as gastric cancer—linked to Helicobacter pylori prevalence—remain prominent, reflecting the epidemiologic transition in which infections and lifestyle-related cancers coexist. Third, hereditary cancer research has gained momentum: population studies of breast cancer have documented a notable frequency of BRCA1 and BRCA2 mutations among high-risk families, and national programs for thalassemia prevention have demonstrated that community-based genetic screening can substantially reduce the birth incidence of a severe hereditary disease. Public health planners therefore confront a dual challenge: expanding early detection infrastructure for common cancers while integrating genetic counseling services into the primary health care system that Iran has long been recognized for.
واژگان کلیدی:
age-standardized rateنرخ استانداردشده سنی؛ برای مقایسه بین جمعیت‌ها
cumulative riskریسک تجمعی؛ خطر تجمع تا سن مشخص
incidenceبروز؛ موارد جدید در واحد زمان
epidemiologic transitionگذار اپیدمیولوژیک؛ هم‌زمانی بیماری‌های عفونی و مزمن
hereditaryارثی؛ خانوادگی با پایه ژنی
momentumشتاب/پویایی روبه‌رشد
birth incidenceشیوع تولد؛ فراوانی تولد نوزادان مبتلا
primary health care (PHC)مراقبت‌های اولیه سلامت؛ نقطه قوت نظام سلامت ایران

1. What is the most common cancer in Iranian women according to the passage?

  • A) stomach cancer
  • B) breast cancer
  • C) lung cancer
  • D) colorectal cancer
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «breast cancer is by far the most common malignancy in women» — by far = با اختلاف زیاد. stomach و colorectal رتبه‌های بعدی‌اند.
استراتژی: در جداول توصیفی، قیدهای رتبه‌بندی (first, followed by, by far) را زیر خط بکشید؛ سوال رتبه‌بندی بسیار رایج است.

2. The passage attributes the prominence of gastric cancer in Iran partly to:

  • A) tobacco advertising
  • B) Helicobacter pylori prevalence
  • C) BRCA mutations
  • D) iodine deficiency
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «infection-related malignancies such as gastric cancer—linked to Helicobacter pylori prevalence—remain prominent» — dash دومیکروبی عامل را روشن می‌کند.
استراتژی: داش‌های توضیحی (—...—) در ریدینگهای آزمون معمولاً حاوی پاسخ‌اند؛ از آن‌ها نپرید.

3. The phrase "amplifying the societal burden" suggests that:

  • A) young patients impose greater indirect costs because illness strikes during productive years
  • B) cancer only affects retired people in Iran
  • C) incidence rates are falling
  • D) the population is aging rapidly
پاسخ: گزینه A
توضیح: منطق متن: ساختار سنی جوان + تشخیص در سال‌های بارور → بار اجتماعی (اقتصادی/خانوادگی) بزرگ‌تر. گزینه ۲ و ۳ با متن ناسازگار؛ ۴ برعکسِ «relatively young age structure».
استراتژی: پیامدهای اجتماعی بیماری در سوالات استنباطی، معمولاً حول بار اقتصادی/کاهش نیروی کار می‌چرخد.

4. The thalassemia prevention program is mentioned as evidence that:

  • A) genetic screening cannot work at the community level
  • B) community-based genetic screening can markedly reduce the birth incidence of a hereditary disease
  • C) Iran lacks primary health care
  • D) cancer screening is impossible
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «national programs for thalassemia prevention have demonstrated that community-based genetic screening can substantially reduce the birth incidence» — demonstrate = نشان دادن؛ مثال تاریخی موفقیت ژنتیک عمومی در ایران.
استراتژی: هرگاه متن یک برنامه ملی را به‌عنوان «demonstrated that...» بیاورد، دقیقاً همان گزاره بعد از that گزینه صحیح است.

5. The "dual challenge" faced by planners consists of:

  • A) reducing the population and banning screening
  • B) expanding early detection for common cancers and integrating genetic counseling into primary care
  • C) treating only infection-related cancers
  • D) importing all genetic services
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله پایانی دو جزء چالش را با while به هم پیوند می‌دهد؛ گزینه صحیح باید هر دو جزء را داشته باشد.
استراتژی: در سوالاتی که «دو جزئی» هستند، گزینه‌ای که فقط یک جزء را می‌گوید (مثلاً فقط غربالگری) ناقص و غلط است.

CRISPR Base Editing: From Bench to Approved Therapy

بر اساس پیشرفت‌های ۲۰۲۳-۲۰۲۵: تأیید exagamglogene autotemcel و موج ویرایش پایه

In late 2023, regulatory authorities approved the first therapy based on CRISPR gene editing, marking a milestone in the treatment of sickle cell disease and transfusion-dependent beta thalassemia. The treatment, known as exagamglogene autotemcel, does not repair the causative mutation directly. Instead, it edits hematopoietic stem cells collected from the patient so that a precisely targeted breakpoint is created in an enhancer of the BCL11A gene specific to erythroid cells. Disrupting this regulatory element reactivates fetal hemoglobin production, which compensates for the defective adult hemoglobin. Because the edited cells are the patient's own, immunological rejection is avoided, although recipients must still undergo conditioning chemotherapy to make room in the marrow. Parallel to nuclease-based editing, a quieter revolution has occurred with base editing, a CRISPR-derived technology that converts one DNA letter directly into another without cutting both strands of the double helix. By avoiding double-strand breaks, base editors substantially reduce the risk of unwanted rearrangements, and investigational therapies have already achieved functional correction of hemoglobinopathies in early trials. Nevertheless, important limitations persist: editing efficiency varies among stem-cell clones, long-term safety data remain limited to a few years of follow-up, off-target effects—though greatly reduced—cannot be excluded entirely, and the current cost places the therapy beyond the reach of most health systems where these diseases are most prevalent. Access, rather than science, is now widely regarded as the principal barrier between gene editing and global equity.
واژگان کلیدی:
gene editingویرایش ژن؛ تغییر هدفمند توالی DNA
enhancerافزاینده؛ عنصر تنظیمی افزایش‌دهنده رونویسی
hematopoietic stem cellsسلول‌های بنیادی خون‌ساز
conditioning chemotherapyشیمی‌درمانی آماده‌سازی؛ خالی‌کردن جای مغز استخوان
base editingویرایش پایه؛ تبدیل مستقیم یک نوکلئوتید بدون برش دو‌رشته
double-strand breakشکست دو‌رشته‌ای DNA
off-target effectsاثرات خارج از هدف؛ ویرایش ناخواسته جایگاه‌های دیگر
equityبرابری/انصاف در دسترسی به سلامت

1. How does exagamglogene autotemcel exert its therapeutic effect?

  • A) by directly repairing the sickle mutation
  • B) by disrupting a BCL11A erythroid enhancer to reactivate fetal hemoglobin
  • C) by replacing bone marrow with donor cells
  • D) by delivering the editing enzymes intravenously without cell collection
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «it edits ... a breakpoint ... in an enhancer of the BCL11A gene specific to erythroid cells. Disrupting this regulatory element reactivates fetal hemoglobin» — هدف غیرمستقیم BCL11A است نه خود جهش.
استراتژی: مکانیسم‌های درمانی جدید را با «هدف» و «اثر» دو ستونه یادداشت کنید؛ سوال mechanism معمولاً یکی از این دو را جابه‌جا می‌کند.

2. The main advantage of base editing over nuclease editing, as stated, is:

استراتژی: در تکنولوژی‌های نو، جمله‌ای که با By avoiding/By reducing شروع می‌شود معمولاً «مزیت» را می‌سازد و مستقیماً سوال می‌شود.

3. The word "conditioning" refers to:

  • B) preparative chemotherapy clearing marrow space
  • C) psychological preparation

پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «must still undergo conditioning chemotherapy to make room in the marrow» — تعریف داخل جمله آمده است.
استراتژی: واژگان تخصصی درمان (conditioning) در پاساژهای ژن‌درمانی همیشه با توضیح همراه می‌آید؛ جمله را تا انتها بخوانید.

4. Which limitation is NOT mentioned in the passage?

  • A) variable editing efficiency among clones
  • B) limited long-term safety follow-up
  • C) residual risk of off-target editing
  • D) complete absence of any therapeutic effect in all patients

5. The final sentence implies that the foremost obstacle to global impact of gene editing is:

  • A) scientific uncertainty
  • B) access and affordability
  • C) ethical opposition
  • D) regulatory approval delays

استراتژی: ساختار rather than در جمله پایانی = مقایسه مستقیم دو عامل؛ یکی برجسته می‌شود و همان جواب سوال است.

Synthetic Lethality: PARP Inhibitors and DNA Repair

بر اساس مباحث روز: PARP inhibition in homologous recombination deficiency

The concept of synthetic lethality arises when simultaneous defects in two genes or pathways kill a cell, whereas a defect in either one alone is tolerable. Tumor cells lacking functional BRCA1 or BRCA2 cannot perform homologous recombination, the high-fidelity pathway that repairs double-strand DNA breaks. They survive, however, by leaning on alternative repair mechanisms, particularly the base excision and single-strand break repair pathways in which the enzyme poly(ADP-ribose) polymerase, or PARP, plays a central role. Pharmacological inhibitors of PARP exploit this dependence: by trapping PARP enzymes at sites of single-strand damage, they prevent replication forks from proceeding, converting unrepaired single-strand lesions into lethal double-strand breaks. In homologous recombination-proficient cells, such breaks would be repaired accurately and the cell would survive; in BRCA-deficient tumor cells, they accumulate catastrophically. This mechanism explains the remarkable selectivity of PARP inhibitors for tumors with germline BRCA mutations, while normal tissues are largely spared. Clinical trials established drugs of this class as effective maintenance therapy in ovarian and breast cancers associated with BRCA mutations or other forms of homologous recombination deficiency, and assays that quantify genomic scarring—cumulative signatures of past repair failure—are now used to identify additional patients who may benefit. Resistance, nonetheless, emerges through several routes, including restoration of homologous recombination by secondary BRCA mutations, replication fork protection, and drug efflux, motivating combination strategies that pair PARP inhibitors with agents targeting these escape pathways.
واژگان کلیدی:
synthetic lethalityمرگ‌ومیری مصنوعی/ترکیبی؛ نقص هم‌زمان دو مسیر
homologous recombinationنوترکیبی همولوگ؛ ترمیم دقیق شکست‌های دو‌رشته
PARPپلی(ADP-ریبوز) پلیمراز؛ ترمیم شکست تک‌رشته
replication forkچنگال همانندسازی

maintenance therapyدرمان نگهدارنده پس از پاسخ اولیه
drug effluxخروج دارو؛ سازوکار مقاومت

1. Synthetic lethality, as defined in the passage, requires that:

  • A) each single defect is independently lethal
  • B) only the combination of both defects is lethal
  • C) both genes are on the same chromosome
  • D) the cell lacks all DNA repair pathways
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «when simultaneous defects in two genes or pathways kill a cell, whereas a defect in either one alone is tolerable» — گزینه ۱ دقیقاً برعکس تعریف است.
استراتژی: تعاریف علمی در جمله اول پاساژ اغلب سوژه سوال تعریف می‌شوند؛ کلمات whereas/while را دقت کنید.

2. How do PARP inhibitors kill BRCA-deficient cells specifically?

  • B) by trapping PARP at single-strand damage, converting lesions into lethal double-strand breaks the cell cannot repair
  • C) by directly cutting both DNA strands
  • D) by inhibiting transcription of PARP mRNA only

3. The term "genomic scarring" is used to describe:

  • C) scars remaining after surgery

استراتژی: اصطلاحات بسته‌بندی‌شده (scarring, synthetic lethality, trapping) همیشه در متن تعریف می‌شوند؛ تعریف را حفظ کنید نه فقط واژه را.

4. Which mechanism of resistance is mentioned?

  • C) increased tumor vascularity
  • D) immune clearance of the drug

استراتژی: سوالات مقاومت دارویی: لیست کوتاه سازوکارها در متن را شماره‌گذارید؛ طراح یکی را برمی‌دارد و سه گزینه از خارج متن می‌سازد.

Liquid Biopsy and Molecular Residual Disease

بر اساس مقالات ۲۰۲۴-۲۰۲۶ درباره ctDNA و MRD

The term liquid biopsy refers to the analysis of tumor-derived molecules—most commonly circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA)—obtained from a simple blood draw rather than from an invasive tissue biopsy. ctDNA fragments, typically around 160 base pairs in length, enter the circulation through the apoptosis of tumor cells and can be detected by deeply sequencing plasma DNA. One of the most consequential applications is the assessment of molecular residual disease (MRD) after apparently curative surgery. Patients in whom ctDNA remains detectable postoperatively have a very high probability of radiographic relapse, often months before conventional imaging reveals any abnormality; conversely, patients who test negative have a substantially lower recurrence risk. This predictive window has encouraged adaptive treatment trials in which adjuvant chemotherapy is escalated for ctDNA-positive patients and de-escalated for ctDNA-negative patients, sparing many from unnecessary toxicity. Despite this promise, important constraints apply. Detection sensitivity depends on tumor burden, location, and shedding biology; certain tumors, notably some brain malignancies, release little DNA across the blood–brain barrier. Assays must be exquisitely specific, because clonal hematopoiesis of indeterminate potential (CHIP)—age-related expansion of mutated blood clones—frequently generates DNA variants in plasma that mimic tumor mutations and can cause false-positive results unless appropriately filtered. Standardization across laboratories remains incomplete, and guidelines accordingly recommend MRD testing within clinical trials or with careful counseling until prospective evidence matures.
واژگان کلیدی:
liquid biopsyبیوپسی مایع؛ نمونه‌برداری خونی به‌جای بافت
circulating tumor DNA (ctDNA)DNA توموری گردان در پلاسما
apoptosisآپوپتوز؛ مرگ سلولی برنامه‌ریزی‌شده
molecular residual disease (MRD)بیماری باقیمانده مولکولی پس از درمان
adjuvant therapyدرمان کمکی پس از جراحی
de-escalateکاهش‌دادن شدت درمان
clonal hematopoiesis (CHIP)خون‌سازی کلونال؛ کلون‌های جهش‌یافته خونی مرتبط با سن
false positiveمثبت کاذب

  • A) about 60 base pairs
  • B) about 160 base pairs
  • C) about 1,600 base pairs
  • D) about 16 base pairs

توضیح: «typically around 160 base pairs in length» — عدد مشخص؛ گزینه‌ها با جابه‌جایی اعشار طراحی شده‌اند.
استراتژی: اعداد را با دقت بخوانید؛ طراحان معمولاً ۰ اضافه یا کم می‌کنند (60/160/1600).

2. Postoperative detection of ctDNA implies that:

  • A) the patient is cured
  • B) relapse is very likely, often preceding radiographic evidence

3. According to the passage, adaptive trials based on ctDNA results aim to:

  • A) give the same chemotherapy to all patients
  • B) escalate treatment for ctDNA-positive and spare ctDNA-negative patients from unnecessary toxicity
  • C) replace surgery with sequencing
  • D) delay treatment until relapse appears on imaging
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «adjuvant chemotherapy is escalated for ctDNA-positive patients and de-escalated for ctDNA-negative patients, sparing many from unnecessary toxicity» — شخصی‌سازی درمان کمکی.
استراتژی: روان مطالب oncology دقیق: «adaptive/escalate/de-escalate» سه واژه کلیدی درمان شخصی‌شده‌اند.

4. Why does CHIP threaten the accuracy of liquid biopsy?

  • A) it destroys ctDNA in the tube
  • B) it produces plasma variants that resemble tumor mutations, causing false positives
  • C) it lowers tumor shedding
  • D) it crosses the blood–brain barrier
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «clonal hematopoiesis ... frequently generates DNA variants in plasma that mimic tumor mutations and can cause false-positive results unless appropriately filtered» — تقلید از جهش تومور.
استراتژی: CHIP در سوالات اخیر همه‌جا هست: تعریف آن (کلون خونی جهش‌یافته وابسته سن) و مشکلش (مثبت کاذب) را یک‌جا حفظ کنید.

5. Which tumor type is noted as poorly suited to ctDNA detection?

  • A) breast cancer
  • B) colorectal cancer
  • C) some brain malignancies
  • D) lung cancer
پاسخ: گزینه C
توضیح: «certain tumors, notably some brain malignancies, release little DNA across the blood–brain barrier» — سد خونی-مغزی مانع آزادسازی DNA است.
استراتژی: هرگز جواب‌ها را از دانش عمومی حدس نزنید؛ «brain/BBB» فقط به این دلیل در متن بود که سوژۀ سوال شود.

Polygenic Risk Scores in Population Screening

بر اساس مقالات ۲۰۲۴-۲۰۲۶ درباره PRS و غربالگری ریسک‌محور

Genome-wide association studies have catalogued hundreds of common variants that individually exert tiny effects on disease susceptibility. A polygenic risk score (PRS) aggregates these effects into a single number that positions an individual along a continuum of inherited risk. For breast cancer, women in the top percentile of the score distribution face a several-fold higher lifetime risk than the population average, comparable in magnitude to some moderate-penetrance monogenic variants. This predictive capacity has fueled proposals for risk-stratified screening: women at high polygenic risk might begin mammography earlier or add annual magnetic resonance imaging, whereas women at low risk might safely delay the onset of screening, reducing overdiagnosis and conserving resources. Formidable obstacles, however, separate theory from implementation. The overwhelming majority of underlying association data derive from populations of European ancestry, and score performance degrades—sometimes dramatically—when applied to other ancestries, a problem known as reduced portability or transferability. Efforts to remedy this include multi-ancestry meta-analysis, larger local reference panels, and ancestry-specific score calibration, all of which require sustained international collaboration and data sharing. Additional concerns include limited communication of probabilistic results to patients, the potential for anxiety or false reassurance, and interactions with non-genetic risk factors such as mammographic density and hormonal exposures. Current guidelines therefore position PRS as an adjunct rather than a replacement for established risk models, pending evidence from prospective interventional studies that stratified screening improves outcomes without widening health inequities.
واژگان کلیدی:
polygenic risk score (PRS)امتیاز ریسک پلی‌ژنیک
susceptibilityحساسیت/مستعدبودن به بیماری
percentileصدک؛ موقعیت نسبی در توزیع
risk-stratified screeningغربالگری طبقه‌بندی‌شده با ریسک
overdiagnosisتشخیص بیش‌ازحد بیماری‌های هرگز علامت‌دارنشدنی
portability/transferabilityانتقال‌پذیری؛ عملکرد مدل در جمعیت دیگر
calibrationکالیبراسیون؛ تطبیق پیش‌بینی با ریسک واقعی
adjunctمکمل؛ ابزار کمکی در کنار روش اصلی

1. A PRS is best described as:

  • A) a diagnostic test for active disease
  • B) an aggregate of many small-effect variants summarizing inherited risk
  • C) a single gene sequencing result
  • D) a measurement of mammographic density
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «aggregates these effects into a single number that positions an individual along a continuum of inherited risk» — خلاصه‌ساز ریسک، نه تشخیص بیماری موجود.
استراتژی: PRS را با «رتبه‌بندی ریسک» ترجمه کنید نه «تشخیص» — گزینه‌های تشخیصی همیشه انحرافی‌اند.

2. In risk-stratified screening, women with low polygenic risk might:

  • A) begin screening earlier than average
  • B) safely delay screening onset, reducing overdiagnosis
  • C) undergo annual MRI
  • D) receive prophylactic surgery
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «women at low risk might safely delay the onset of screening, reducing overdiagnosis and conserving resources» — منطق دو‌طرفه تخصیص منابع.
استراتژی: دو سمت طیف ریسک را جدول کنید: high → earlier/MRI؛ low → delay — سوال معمولاً یک سمت را می‌پرسد.

3. The term "transferability" refers to:

  • A) the portability of laboratory equipment
  • B) how well a score performs when applied to ancestries other than those in which it was developed
  • C) the speed of sequencing
  • D) the ability to transfer patients between hospitals
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «score performance degrades ... when applied to other ancestries, a problem known as reduced portability or transferability» — تعریف داخل جمله.
استراتژی: مفاهیم bias/عدالت در ژنومیکس جمعیتی از موضوعات محبوب جدید است؛ تعریف transferability را حفظ کنید.

4. Which strategy is mentioned as a remedy for the ancestry problem?

  • A) abandoning PRS entirely
  • B) multi-ancestry meta-analysis and ancestry-specific calibration
  • C) restricting PRS to men
  • D) ignoring non-genetic risk factors
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «Efforts to remedy this include multi-ancestry meta-analysis, larger local reference panels, and ancestry-specific score calibration».
استراتژی: سوالات «solution/remedy»: جملات شامل Efforts to remedy/include را هدف بگیرید.

5. According to current guidelines mentioned in the passage, PRS should be regarded as:

  • A) a replacement for established risk models
  • B) an adjunct to them
  • C) a screening test for all newborns
  • D) a tool only for researchers, never clinicians
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «position PRS as an adjunct rather than a replacement for established risk models» — همان ساختار X rather than Y.
استراتژی: adjunct = مکمل؛ در متون راهنمایی، وضعیت نوظهورها تقریباً همیشه «adjunct pending evidence» است.

Personalized Neoantigen Cancer Vaccines

بر اساس فاز ۲b/۳ mRNA-4157 و پیشرفت‌های ۲۰۲۴-۲۰۲۶

Unlike infectious disease vaccines, which present identical antigens to an entire population, personalized cancer vaccines are manufactured for a single patient from the unique mutations of that patient's tumor. The workflow begins with whole-exome and RNA sequencing of tumor and normal tissue, followed by computational pipelines that nominate candidate neoantigens—mutated peptides predicted to bind the patient's human leukocyte antigen (HLA) molecules and to be recognized by T lymphocytes. Twenty to thirty-four selected epitopes are then encoded into a delivery vehicle, most prominently messenger RNA encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles, the same platform proven at global scale by COVID-19 vaccines. In randomized studies of resected high-risk melanoma, the combination of an individualized neoantigen vaccine with the immune checkpoint inhibitor pembrolizumab significantly prolonged recurrence-free survival compared with pembrolizumab alone, and similar strategies are advancing in pancreatic cancer, non-small cell lung cancer, and colorectal tumors. Formidable logistical hurdles nonetheless remain: manufacturing each patient's bespoke product takes weeks, requires deep tumor sequencing of sufficient quality, and depends on reliable algorithms whose predictions of immunogenicity remain imperfect. Tumors with low mutation burden furnish few candidate neoantigens, limiting applicability, and glucocorticoid use around infusion has been associated with diminished immune responses. Whether the approach will extend beyond mutation-rich tumors, and whether health systems can absorb its cost, are questions that ongoing Phase III trials are now positioned to answer.
واژگان کلیدی:
neoantigenنئوآنتی‌ژن؛ پپتید نوظهور از جهش تومور
whole-exome sequencingتوالی‌یابی کل اگزوم
epitopeاپیتوپ؛ بخش آنتی‌ژن قابل شناسایی توسط ایمنی
lipid nanoparticleنانوذره لیپیدی؛ حامل mRNA
checkpoint inhibitorمهارکننده چک‌پوینت ایمنی
recurrence-free survivalبقای بدون عود؛ نقطه پایانی کارآزمایی
mutation burdenبار جهشی؛ تعداد جهش‌های سوماتیک
bespokeسفارشی‌دوز؛ ساخت اختصاصی برای یک فرد

1. What distinguishes personalized cancer vaccines from conventional vaccines?

  • A) they use killed whole tumor cells
  • B) each vaccine is built from the unique mutations of an individual patient's tumor
  • C) they target infectious agents
  • D) they are identical across the population
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «Unlike infectious disease vaccines, which present identical antigens to an entire population, personalized cancer vaccines are manufactured for a single patient from the unique mutations...» — جمله اول تقابل را کامل می‌کند.
استراتژی: جمله اول پاساژ با Unlike شروع می‌شود → تفاوت دو مفهوم؛ در سوال تفاوت، همین جمله جواب است.

2. A candidate neoantigen must satisfy which prediction requirement?

  • A) binding to the patient's HLA molecules and recognition by T lymphocytes
  • B) secretion into plasma
  • C) presence in the germline genome
  • D) inhibition of lipid nanoparticles
پاسخ: گزینه A
توضیح: «mutated peptides predicted to bind the patient's HLA molecules and to be recognized by T lymphocytes» — دو شرط: اتصال HLA و شناسایی T.
استراتژی: پپتید-اتصال HLA-شناسایی T لمفوسیت سه‌گانه ثابت ایمیونولوژی واکسن است.

3. The vaccine platform highlighted in the passage is:

  • A) inactivated virus
  • B) mRNA in lipid nanoparticles
  • C) live attenuated bacteria
  • D) recombinant protein with alum
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «most prominently messenger RNA encapsulated in lipid nanoparticles, the same platform proven at global scale by COVID-19 vaccines».
استراتژی: پیوند فناوری واکسن کووید با واکسن سرطان نکته محبوب متن‌های جدید است.

4. Tumors with low mutation burden are a challenge because:

  • A) they grow too fast to sequence
  • B) they provide few candidate neoantigens
  • C) they do not respond to pembrolizumab under any circumstances
  • D) their HLA molecules are absent
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «Tumors with low mutation burden furnish few candidate neoantigens, limiting applicability» — furnish = فراهم‌کردن.
استراتژی: furnish/provide/yield واژه‌های مترادف «فراهم‌آوردن»اند؛ در سوال علت، شرط کمبود ماده اولیه (neoantigen) را دنبال کنید.

5. The passage indicates that Phase III trials will primarily clarify:

  • A) the mechanism of lipid nanoparticle formation
  • B) broader applicability, outcomes, and affordability for health systems
  • C) the history of vaccine platforms
  • D) methods of tumor resection
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله پایانی دو پرسش باز را نام می‌برد: تعمیم به تومورهای کم‌جهش و هزینه/جذب توسط نظام سلامت — دقیقاً محور فاز ۳.
استراتژی: جمله پایانی با Whether...or whether ساختار «پرسش‌های باز آینده» می‌سازد؛ سوالی درباره نقش فاز ۳ از همین می‌آید.

Artificial Intelligence in Structural Genomics

بر اساس AlphaFold 2/3 و کاربردهای ۲۰۲۴-۲۰۲۶

For half a century, determining the three-dimensional structure of a protein required years of laboratory work using X-ray crystallography or nuclear magnetic resonance spectroscopy. Deep-learning systems have now largely overturned that bottleneck. AlphaFold, first demonstrated at an international structure-prediction contest, predicts protein structures from amino acid sequences with accuracy approaching experimental methods for a large fraction of targets, and its successor extends predictions to complexes of proteins with DNA, RNA, and small ligands. Structural biologists now routinely consult predicted models when designing experiments, docking studies, and mutagenesis strategies, and databases containing hundreds of millions of predicted structures have become public resources. The impact on rare disease genetics is particularly noteworthy: for variants of uncertain significance in genes lacking structural data, predicted models permit researchers to map substitutions onto three-dimensional space, distinguish those disrupting buried hydrophobic cores or catalytic residues from benign surface changes, and thereby prioritize variants for functional assays. Enthusiasm must nevertheless be tempered by genuine limitations. Predicted confidence varies across regions; intrinsically disordered segments, alternative conformations, and dynamic complexes remain difficult; and ligand-binding pockets—central to drug discovery—may be modeled with less reliability than protein backbones. Nor does a structure, predicted or experimental, by itself explain a biological mechanism; integration with biochemical, genetic, and clinical data remains indispensable. Within these boundaries, machine-learning structure prediction has shifted from a computational curiosity to an everyday instrument of molecular biology and medical genetics.
واژگان کلیدی:
crystallographyبلورشناسی پرتو ایکس؛ روش تعیین ساختار
deep learningیادگیری عمیق؛ شبکه‌های عصبی چندلایه
dockingداکینگ؛ شبیه‌سازی اتصال لیگاند به پروتئین
mutagenesisجهش‌زایی هدفمند؛ طراحی جهش‌های آزمایشی
variant of uncertain significance (VUS)واریانت با اهمیت نامشخص
hydrophobic coreهسته هیدروفوبیک داخل پروتئین
catalytic residueباقیمانده کاتالیزی؛ اسیدآمینه فعال آنزیم
intrinsically disorderedدر嫁给 بی‌نظم ذاتی؛ بخش‌های بدون ساختار ثابت

1. The main contribution of AlphaFold described in the passage is:

  • A) replacing all laboratory experiments
  • B) predicting protein structures from sequence at near-experimental accuracy for many targets
  • C) sequencing genomes faster
  • D) curing genetic diseases directly
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «predicts protein structures from amino acid sequences with accuracy approaching experimental methods for a large fraction of targets» — گزینه ۱ مطلق‌سازی غلط است؛ متن گزینه ۳/۴ را نمی‌گوید.
استراتژی: درباره AI همیشه مراقب گزینه‌های اغراق‌آمیز (replace all/cure/directly) باشید؛ متن‌های علمی محتاط‌اند.

2. How do predicted structures aid rare disease genetics?

  • A) by replacing genetic testing
  • B) by mapping missense variants onto 3D space to distinguish structure-disrupting from benign changes
  • C) by increasing mutation rates
  • D) by automating patient interviews
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «permit researchers to map substitutions onto three-dimensional space, distinguish those disrupting buried hydrophobic cores or catalytic residues from benign surface changes, and thereby prioritize variants» — کاربرد VUS تفسیری.
استراتژی: پیوند AI-VUS یکی از داغ‌ترین موضوعات ژنتیک بالینی جدید است؛ جمله‌اش را در متن هدف بگیرید.

3. Which aspect is described as modeled less reliably?

  • A) protein backbones
  • B) ligand-binding pockets
  • C) amino acid sequences
  • D) hydrogen atoms
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: «ligand-binding pockets—central to drug discovery—may be modeled with less reliability than protein backbones».
استراتژی: مقایسه‌های less/more reliable را علامت بزنید؛ سوال مقایسه‌ای می‌آید.

4. The phrase "tempered by genuine limitations" indicates that the author:

  • A) rejects the technology
  • B) presents a balanced view acknowledging both value and constraints
  • C) works for a competing company
  • D) doubts the existence of proteins
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: temper = تعدیل‌کردن؛ نویسنده بعد از ستایش، محدودیت‌های واقعی را فهرست می‌کند → لحن متعادل علمی.
استراتژی: temper/moderate/qualify هم‌خانواده‌اند: «تعادل‌دهی به ادعا» — نشان لحن balanced.

5. The passage concludes that machine-learning prediction now functions as:

  • A) a computational curiosity
  • B) an everyday instrument of molecular biology and medical genetics
  • C) a substitute for clinical judgment
  • D) an unproven hypothesis
پاسخ: گزینه B
توضیح: جمله آخر: «has shifted from a computational curiosity to an everyday instrument of molecular biology and medical genetics» — همان ساختار from X to Y.
استراتژی: جمله پایانی از X to Y تقابل گذشته/حاضر را می‌سازد؛ سوالی درباره وضعیت فعلی، بازتاب مستقیم Y است.

جدول واژگان پرتکرار زبان تخصصی ژنتیک در آزمون‌های دکتری

این واژگان از تحلیل ریدینگ‌های آزمون‌های سال‌های قبل استخراج شده‌اند؛ مرور روز امتحان توصیه می‌شود.
